### 2026-10-08 09:09:28  #公司

【兩部門：推進醫保支付方式改革，完善特例單議等配套機制】國家衛生健康委、國家發展改革委印發《醫療康復護理擴容提升工程實施方案》。其中提到，完善多元支付體系。按程式將療效確切、群眾急需的康復護理類醫療服務專案納入基本醫療保險支付範圍。推進醫保支付方式改革，完善特例單議等配套機制，對接受康復護理服務的短期住院患者實行按病種付費，對接受康復護理服務的長期住院患者探索實行按床日付費等支付方式。鼓勵商業保險公司開發康復護理相關保險產品，並與基本醫療保險、長期護理保險、工傷保險做好銜接。

### 2026-10-08 09:07:35  #其他

【兩部門：加快腦機介面、具身智慧、仿生驅動等前沿技術佈局】國家衛生健康委、國家發展改革委印發《醫療康復護理擴容提升工程實施方案》。其中提到，加強創新產品研發和應用。鼓勵行業優勢企業、高校、科研院所、醫療衛生機構建設產學研用創新聯合體，加大創新成果轉化力度。圍繞神經康復、運動康復、創面修復等重點領域，加快腦機介面、具身智慧、仿生驅動等前沿技術佈局，支援智慧康復裝置、植介入器械、新型生物材料等關鍵技術攻關。研發融合中醫理論與現代技術的中醫智慧康復護理產品。推動將符合條件的康復護理創新醫療器械、藥物按程式納入優先審評審批範圍。鼓勵高水平醫院發揮引領作用，總結腦機介面等產品應用經驗，強化康復護理創新成果在醫療衛生機構、社群、家庭等場景落地應用。

### 2026-10-08 09:07:03  #其他

【兩部門：制定並推廣一批中醫康復護理方案和技術規範】國家衛生健康委、國家發展改革委印發《醫療康復護理擴容提升工程實施方案》。其中提到，發揮中醫藥獨特優勢。聚焦中醫特色優勢病種，制定並推廣一批中醫康復護理方案和技術規範。鼓勵有條件的醫療衛生機構積極運用中醫藥技術方法開展康復護理服務，推動康復醫院更好發揮中醫康復治療室功能作用，強化基層醫療衛生機構針灸、推拿等中醫適宜技術應用。支援有資質的其他機構提供中醫康復護理服務。推廣普及中醫養生保健方法。促進中醫藥、中華傳統體育與現代康復護理技術融合。

### 2026-10-08 09:06:20  #其他

【兩部門：推進康復護理能力條件建設 國家優先支援城區常住人口100萬以上的地級及以上城市實施醫療康復護理擴容提升工程】國家衛生健康委、國家發展改革委印發《醫療康復護理擴容提升工程實施方案》。其中提到，推進康復護理能力條件建設。以地市為單元，加強康復護理服務能力建設，支援二級及以上醫院合理設定康復護理病房或功能專區、配置專業裝置，支援基層醫療衛生機構加強基本康復護理能力建設、配置適合家庭病床服務的行動式裝置。國家優先支援城區常住人口100萬以上的地級及以上城市實施醫療康復護理擴容提升工程。將縣域醫療衛生機構康復護理能力建設納入醫療衛生強基工程統籌支援。推動婦幼保健機構、兒童醫院、精神衛生機構、職業病防治機構等其他醫療衛生機構圍繞提供專業特色康復護理服務，配置康復評估、訓練等裝置，拓展康復護理功能，補齊設施裝置短板。

### 2026-10-08 09:00:10  #公司

【保時捷B級SUV新車將於2028年量產，與純電Macan並行銷售】10月7日，保時捷在資本市場日上披露，純電動718 Boxster和Cayman車型預計將在2028年首個完整生產年度助力銷售。2028年，保時捷還將推出一款B級全新SUV車型，並與當前純電動Macan並行銷售。這款搭載內燃機和插電式混合動力系統的新車型，在2028年量產爬坡後，預計將於2029年對銷量和盈利能力帶來顯著貢獻。此後，公司還將推出更多新產品，主要集中在利潤率較高的D級和E級細分市場，預計將進一步提升盈利水平。

### 2026-10-08 08:26:09  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Inflation Expectations #Importance 2

Australia Inflation Expectations Hit 4-Month High — Australia’s consumer inflation expectations rose to 5.3% in October 2026, up from 4.9% in the prior two months and reaching their highest level since June. The outlook was reinforced by August data showing trimmed mean CPI remained elevated at 3.6% year-on-year, its highest since September 2024 and above the Reserve Bank of Australia’s 2–3% target range. At its October meeting, the RBA unanimously raised the cash rate by 25bps to 4.6%, its fourth hike this year and lifting borrowing costs to their highest level since 2011. Policymakers said inflation remained elevated, with some upside risks identified in August now materialising. They also noted that strong AI-related demand is driving rapid increases in global prices for technology-related goods. Still, the board said monetary policy was well placed to respond to developments, while remaining focused on its mandate of price stability and full employment.

### 2026-10-08 08:25:47  #公司 #觀點 #央行 #國際

【中金：節後投資者信心有望逐步修復，A股或迎“開門紅”】中金公司研報表示，展望節後，A股或迎來“開門紅”。節前A股在多項外部因素影響下表現偏弱，假期期間海外市場漲多跌少，疊加近期國內經濟資料較平穩，節後三季報有望為市場帶來一定的業績支撐，預計10月投資者信心有望修復，節後A股有望迎來“開門紅”。行業配置上，建議關注兩條主線：1. 景氣成長：AI產業鏈的硬體環節業績普遍高增長，但隨著敘事的變化未來或將面臨分化，部分壁壘不高、產能投放進展較快的環節風險較大，而需求確定性高且產能瓶頸難以緩解的領域有望繼續受益，建議關注光通訊、半導體裝置，以及上游電力瓶頸相關行業。AI產業鏈以外，創新藥（尤其是CXO）、電網裝置整體景氣度較高。2. 週期改善：越來越多領域基本面正在從週期底部回升，建議結合產能週期視角關注供需格局改善的領域，如化工、石化和工程機械等行業，有色金屬各領域基本面較好，但需關注美聯儲緊縮風險對金融屬性的影響。

【從搶著買到打折賣，越南樓市又“變天”？】最近，越南地產股再次跌入債市爆雷後的“至暗時刻”。截至9月30日，越南VN30指數週跌2.46%，多隻熱門地產股猛烈下挫，跌至2022年以來的最低位。2022年，堪稱越南房地產行業近十年來“最悲壯”的一年——大批地產商債務違約，資金鍊瞬間斷裂，破產、清盤輪番上演，留給購房者的只剩數千個爛尾樓專案。如今，越南地產股再次跌進2022年以來的低位，是否預示著危機正捲土重來？近日，記者實地走訪河內、胡志明市的樓盤和中介機構，發現越南房地產業確實再次回到了相似的歷史場景：信貸收縮，流動性緊張，從貸款利率到債券收益率都在大漲——這是一場對樓盤去化和房企現金流的嚴峻考驗。但和幾年前不同的是，行業經歷過一輪出清，能繼續留在牌桌的都是體格更健壯的大玩家；國際產業轉移還在繼續，外資加速佈局越南，房地產的買盤在擴大；政府親歷過崩盤的風浪，正試圖用行政手段和貨幣政策控制過快的擴張節奏。交易量、去化週期、二手房價格……種種資料都反映出今年越南樓市正在降溫。（21財經）

Meanwhile, oil prices advanced following reports that the Trump administration had asked the Pentagon to prepare strike options against Iran that could be carried out before the midterm elections. Risks to shipping through the Strait of Hormuz also remain elevated, even as oil flows from the Middle East are estimated to have recovered to prewar levels.

Australian Stocks Trade Lower After Wall Street Losses — Australian shares slipped 26 points, or 0.3%, to 8,701 in early Thursday trade, extending the prior session’s drop as subdued U.S. stock futures followed Wall Street’s break from recent winning streaks overnight. Locally, October inflation expectations rose to a four-month high, reflecting persistent price pressures amid elevated global energy costs linked to Middle East tensions. Tech, non-energy minerals, and logistics drove weakness, though gains in consumer non-durables, energy minerals, and retail trade limited downside. 【CITIC Securities: oil-shipping cycle reshapes; monitor express and cross-border peak season】CITIC Securities says 3Q26 transport & logistics earnings divergence should persist as a cycle paradigm shift boosts profit elasticity in oil tankers and container lines. Oil shipping: expect a renewed upswing in crude tanker spot rates after the National Day holiday; recommend names positioned to benefit and monitor G7 strategic crude/diesel releases, elevated refining cracking spreads, and seasonal plus cross-regional demand that should lift product tanker volumes. Container shipping: shipowners’ margin recovery is the likely valuation catalyst; recommended exposure to Red Sea re-rating plays and carriers with stable cash flow and high dividend capacity. Express delivery: peak-season price recovery underway, while low volume growth is accelerating competitive differentiation. Air cargo: AI-related supply-chain shipments were a key driver of 1H air freight growth and have raised the share of schedule-stable, high-tech cargo; watch peak-season restocking and new capacity flowing through to margins and firms with low expectations but attractive upside for cyclic resilience.

### 2026-10-08 07:59:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Current Account #Importance 2

Japan Current Account Surplus Above Forecasts — Japan’s current account surplus rose to JPY 4,062.0 billion in August 2026 from JPY 3,628.1 billion in the same month a year earlier, surpassing market expectations for a JPY 3,194 billion surplus. Primary income surplus increased to JPY 5,133.2 billion from JPY 4,222.7 billion a year earlier, while the services deficit narrowed to JPY 115.8 billion from JPY 266.7 billion and the secondary income deficit shrank to JPY 267.7 billion from JPY 441.7 billion. Meanwhile, the goods account shifted to a deficit of JPY 687.7 billion from a surplus of JPY 113.8 billion, as exports rose 16.9%, largely supported by strong demand for AI-related electronics, but imports grew faster at 26.7%, driven partly by higher crude oil purchases. In the first half of 2026, Japan's trade surplus widened to JPY 17.43 trillion from JPY 14.23 trillion in the same period a year earlier.

US Futures Edge Higher as Yields Stabilize — US stock futures edged higher on Thursday as the recent surge in Treasury yields paused, with the 10-year and 30-year yields settling below 5.3% and 5.7%, respectively. In regular trading on Wednesday, the Dow fell 0.66%, while both the S&P 500 and Nasdaq Composite slipped 0.22%. The declines came as Treasury yields climbed to their highest levels since 2002, with the 10-year and 30-year yields reaching intraday highs of 5.365% and 5.732%, respectively. Elevated oil prices also continued to fuel inflation concerns amid fears of a potential escalation between the US and Iran and persistent threats to shipping through the Strait of Hormuz. In corporate news, Levi Strauss fell nearly 2% in extended trading after the retailer cut its full-year revenue growth outlook. Investors now await additional earnings reports on Thursday from PepsiCo, Progressive and Delta Air Lines, along with the latest weekly jobless claims figures.

【險資可投港股通ETF 跨境配置再添新通道】保險資金投資港股通ETF的監管口徑進一步明確。記者獲悉，多家險企近日收到《關於明確保險資金投資港股通ETF監管口徑的函》。函件明確，按照監管規定可投資港股通股票的保險機構，可以投資港股通ETF，並參照保險資金投資港股通股票的相關監管規定執行，該監管口徑自9月20日起實施。8月18日，國家金融監督管理總局表態支援內地保險機構透過滬深港通投資香港交易所買賣基金；9月23日，香港證監會行政總裁梁鳳儀公開證實，內地保險資金已正式登陸ETF通。Wind資料顯示，截至9月末，納入港股通範圍的ETF共31只，合計規模3219億元，全部為股票型。金融監管總局資料顯示，截至2026年二季度末，險資運用餘額突破40萬億元，其中投向股票和證券投資基金合計6.39萬億元，較年初增長6907億元。這意味著，在港股通股票之外，符合條件的險資新增一類標準化、透明度較高的港股配置工具，跨境配置通道進一步拓寬。（經濟參考報）

Peru Unexpectedly Holds Rate at 4.25% — Peru’s Central Reserve Bank unexpectedly kept its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 4.25% in October 2026, extending its pause for a 13th consecutive meeting, defying expectations of a 25 bps hike. Annual inflation accelerated to 4.5% in September from 4.4% in August, marking the seventh consecutive month above the central bank’s 1%-3% target range due to higher fuel and electricity prices. Meanwhile, inflation excluding food and energy remained at 4.5%. Excluding transportation, underlying inflation stood at 1.8% and has remained below 2% since April 2025. Twelve-month inflation expectations stood at 3.1%, slightly above the upper limit of the target range. The central bank expects inflation to return toward 2% as supply shocks fade. However, the risks of El Niño and Middle East tensions could have persistent effects on inflation. Current-conditions indicators were mixed in September, while expectations indicators remained in optimistic territory, albeit with some moderation.

### 2026-10-08 07:15:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Colombia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Colombia Inflation Hits Over 2-Year High — The annual inflation rate in Colombia rose to 6.29% in September 2026 from 6.24% in August, falling short of market expectations of 6.32% but marking the highest reading since July 2024. The latest figure pushed further above the central bank’s target of 3%, driven by stronger price growth for furniture and household maintenance (6.75% vs 6.67% in August), transport (5.27% vs 5.25%), food and non-alcoholic beverages (6.74% vs 6.10%), education (7.49% vs 7.45%), and restaurants and hotels (9.44% vs 9.36%). Housing and utilities inflation was unchanged at 5.74%. Meanwhile, price growth eased for information and communication (4.45% vs 5.97%), recreation and culture (4.91% vs 4.98%), miscellaneous goods and services (5.63% vs 6.05%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (6.05% vs 6.94%), clothing and footwear (3.12% vs 3.26%), and health (7.79% vs 8.30%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose by 0.37% in September, slowing from a 0.39% gain in the preceding period.

### 2026-10-08 07:12:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #RICS House Price Balance #Importance 2

UK House Price Balance Weakens to 3-Month Low — The RICS UK Residential Market Survey showed that the house price balance deteriorated to -32% in September 2026 from -28% in August, which was the highest in five months and worse than market expectations of -30%. It was the lowest figure since June and ended a four-month period during which the indicator had been becoming progressively less negative. The net balance for new buyer enquiries fell to -22%, while the sales expectations balance declined to -6%. London reported a weaker price balance than the national figure, while the North price balance continued to rise. The three-month house price expectations balance stood at -24%, signalling expectations of further near-term pressure, but stabilisation over a 12-month horizon. “A renewed rise in interest rate expectations has created a fresh headwind for the housing market, with buyers becoming a little more cautious and sales activity losing some momentum this month,” said RICS Head of Market Research and Analysis Tarrant Parsons.

### 2026-10-08 07:06:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Korea #Current Account #Importance 1

South Korea Current Account Surplus Widens — South Korea’s current account surplus widened to USD 46.11 billion in August 2026, up from USD 42.08 billion in July. The goods account surplus increased to USD 46.81 billion in August from USD 40.43 billion in July, with exports surging 82.1% yoy to USD 104.80 billion, while imports climbed 23.8% to USD 57.99 billion. Exports surged, boosted by strong growth in semiconductor exports amid the global AI boom. Additionally, the services account deficit narrowed to USD 1.68 billion from USD 1.97 billion, due to smaller deficits in the use of intellectual property, while the transportation surplus increased. Meanwhile, the primary income account surplus narrowed to USD 1.92 billion from USD 4.35 billion, while the secondary income deficit increased to USD 0.94 billion from USD 0.73 billion, reflecting higher secondary income payments. For the first eight months of 2026, the current account surplus jumped to USD 279.20 billion from USD 69.67 billion in the same period of 2025.

【人民日報評論員觀察：中國市場的稀缺性從何來】當今世界，最稀缺的資源是市場。中國市場又常被投資者認為是“稀缺中的稀缺”。“穩”從何而來？制度優勢是壓艙石。市場和政府同向發力。活力與秩序兼顧統籌。“堅持有效市場和有為政府相結合”，既是社會主義市場經濟體制的顯著優勢，也是“十五五”時期經濟社會發展必須遵循的重要原則。持續提升宏觀經濟治理能力，推動傳統產業轉型升級、新興產業蓄勢壯大、未來產業前瞻佈局，中國市場的確定性、稀缺性還將更加突出。中國經濟之“穩”，不是靜態的，而是一種前進勢態中的高水平動態平衡。這份“穩”的背後，是深沉的戰略定力和積極的歷史主動。

【High pay for Strait of Hormuz transits as crews accept ~5% attack risk】A captain described operational changes to oil and LNG shipments through the Strait of Hormuz amid Iranian threats: night-only runs, strict equipment limits (no lights or mobile phones, typically only one radar), navigation by the Oman coast and lighthouses, and AIS cutters switched off at departure. Crews — largely from the Philippines and India — are being paid premiums, with some contracts shortened but paying several times normal wages and some owners offering an extra six months’ pay; decks are being reinforced with sandbags to guard against drones or missiles. Shipping executives say the chance of being attacked while transiting under US military surveillance is about 1 in 20; Iran has compiled a blacklist of tankers involved in the trade.

OPT allows F-1 students to work in roles directly related to their field of study and is a key transition pathway to employment visas. DHS said the measure is intended to curb visa fraud and abuse and to prevent OPT from becoming a backdoor into the US labor market.

Brazil Vehicle Production Falls in September — Brazil’s vehicle production fell 1.1% month-over-month to 268,300 units in September 2026, following a 6.8% increase in August. Dollar Index at March 2025-Highs — The dollar index traded around 102.3 on Wednesday, its highest level since March 2025, as volatility in oil markets continued to fuel inflation concerns, reinforcing expectations that the Fed may need to keep monetary policy tighter for longer. Meanwhile, minutes from the September FOMC meeting showed that most Fed officials supported another rate hike this year, although they signalled no urgency to act. The odds of a rate hike in October fell to 17%, from around 70% in the days following the September decision, while the probability of a 25bps hike in December edged up to around 70%. The greenback strengthened broadly, gaining the most against the euro, as political and fiscal concerns in Spain and France weighed on the common currency.

### 2026-10-08 02:27:14  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 2

US 10-Year Yield Eases From 24-Year High — The yield on the 10-year Treasury note was at 5.28%, 8 bps below the 24-year high touched earlier in the session as oil prices swung lower and softened the alarm over energy inflation. OPEC+ shipped laden tankers through the Persian Gulf to limit the crunch for global refiners. This coincided with a strong auction for 10-year notes, which stopped through by 1.7 bps. Still, long-maturity yields were close to their highest since the start of the millennium as the risk of high inflation was combined with robust growth, widening budget deficits, and soaring levels of corporate debt supply. Minutes from the Federal Reserve's last meeting showed strong consensus within the FOMC that higher rates are necessary and the labor market is at full employment. Policymakers also noted that the Treasury market is functioning well, but the central bank must be alert to frictions due to high yields. Over 80% of the rate futures market is positioned for at least one more rate hike by the end of the year.

Brent Crude Dips on Easing Supply Concerns — Brent crude eased below the $101-per-barrel mark on Wednesday, extending a slide from September's two-month peak on signs of easing Middle Eastern supply risks. Saudi Arabia cut official selling prices for its flagship crude grade to $5 below the regional benchmark as tankers were able to depart the Persian Gulf with full cargoes. Evidence of a pickup in export volumes from regional OPEC+ producers coincided with reports that US and Iranian officials were holding talks with regional authorities this week. Further easing supply concerns, Saudi Arabia said its East-West pipeline was operating at full capacity, diverting up to 5.8 million bpd of crude to the Red Sea. Still, shipping costs remained elevated following reported strikes against vessels in the Strait of Hormuz. Stateside, EIA data showed crude stockpiles fell by over 3 million barrels last week, while the Strategic Petroleum Reserve remained pinned near multi-decade lows.

Fed Likely to Raise Rates Again This Year — Most Fed policymakers judged that another increase in the target range for the federal funds rate would likely be appropriate by year-end. However, they emphasized that they approached each meeting with an open mind and that future decisions would depend on incoming economic data, its implications for the outlook, and the balance of risks, according to the minutes of the September 15–16, 2026 FOMC meeting. Minutes also showed that all policymakers supported raising the target range for the federal funds rate by 25bps to 3.75%-4%. Participants generally emphasized that inflation remained elevated while the labor market appeared to be near full employment, with some signs of strengthening, and that economic activity was expanding at a solid pace. Furthermore, almost all participants assessed that, while inflation risks were tilted to the upside, risks to the labor market had diminished and were now broadly balanced.

【五名前巴克萊交易員操縱基準利率案定罪被英國上訴法院推翻】五名前巴克萊交易員曾在一系列具有標誌性的欺詐案中被判操縱基準利率罪名成立，如今其定罪已被倫敦一家法院推翻。英國上訴法院法官撤銷了Alex Pabon、Jay Merchant和Jonathan Mathew的定罪。三人2016年因協助操縱倫敦銀行間同業拆借利率（Libor）被判串謀欺詐罪成立併入獄。另外兩名前巴克萊交易員Philippe Moryoussef和Colin Bermingham分別於2018年和2019年被判操縱歐元銀行間同業拆借利率（Euribor）罪名成立。此前，前瑞銀集團交易員Tom Hayes的定罪已被英國最高法院推翻。Hayes是首名因操縱利率被定罪的人士，也是其中最受矚目的人物之一。最高法院法官裁定，Hayes案審判中陪審團受到錯誤指引，他和另一名交易員Carlo Palombo應被宣告無罪。

Gasoline Futures Drop — US gasoline futures fell below $3.35 per gallon, snapping a two-day winning streak amid prospects of higher fuel supplies. EIA data showed US gasoline inventories unexpectedly rose by 382 thousand barrels in the week ending October 2nd, rebounding after two consecutive draws. The planned G7 release of emergency crude and diesel reserves could also ease supply pressures, while gasoline prices at the pump have retreated from recent peaks as demand weakens. However, a storm in the Gulf of Mexico threatened to disrupt refinery operations, with Gulf Coast refineries accounting for about half of US refining capacity, which stands at 18.2 million barrels per day. Meanwhile, continued attacks in the Middle East kept energy-market risks elevated despite improvement in regional shipments. Russia’s ongoing restrictions on diesel exports and refinery disruptions, alongside China’s reported suspension of some October fuel exports, also tightened fuel markets.

### 2026-10-08 00:47:32  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Palladium Falls to 1-Year Low — Palladium futures fell below $1,230 an ounce to their lowest level since September 2025, pressured by investment flows and a weaker demand outlook. Elevated energy prices have fueled concerns over persistent inflation and debt affordability, pushing sovereign yields to multi-year highs and increasing the opportunity cost of holding non-yielding assets such as palladium. Additional supply could also weigh on prices, as a state-owned firm in Zimbabwe, the world’s third-largest platinum-group metal reserves, plans to develop a mining project in Darwendale next year. Over the longer term, however, rapid AI infrastructure expansion could support demand. Adding to the bearish pressure in the long-term, UBS forecasts a market surplus for the metal, as a decline in automotive demand is expected to more than offset lower mined output in South Africa, where elevated operating costs and power grid instability remain concerns, and Russia, where ongoing trade risks continue to weigh on supply.

【Hormuz shipping disruption lifts VLCC freight to $77 mln】Shipping disruption in the Strait of Hormuz has sharply raised global tanker supply-chain costs. Baltic Exchange data show VLCC charter rates to carry U.S. crude to Asia have climbed to $77 mln, versus a 2025 average of $9.2 mln. On a 2.0m bbl cargo this implies roughly $38.5/bbl in transport cost; hostilities involving Iran have altered Middle East export routes and shipping modes — flows have partly resumed but longer voyages and a reduced effective fleet are keeping freight elevated.

Crude Oil Eases to 1-Month Low — Crude oil eased below the $89-per-barrel mark on Wednesday, marking its lowest level in over a month and extending a 16% slide from September's four-month peak on signs of easing Middle Eastern supply risks. Saudi Arabia cut official selling prices for its flagship crude grade to $5 below the regional benchmark as tankers were able to depart the Persian Gulf with full cargoes. Evidence of a pickup in export volumes from regional OPEC+ producers coincided with reports that US and Iranian officials were holding talks with regional authorities this week. Further easing supply concerns, Saudi Arabia said its East-West pipeline was operating at full capacity, diverting up to 5.8 million bpd of crude to the Red Sea. Still, shipping costs remained elevated following reported strikes against vessels in the Strait of Hormuz. Stateside, EIA data showed crude stockpiles fell by over 3 million barrels last week, while the Strategic Petroleum Reserve remained pinned near multi-decade lows.

Platinum Falls to Nine-Week Low — Platinum futures fell below $1,700 an ounce to their lowest level in just over two months, pressured by investment flows and a weaker demand outlook. Elevated energy prices have fueled concerns over persistent inflation and debt affordability, pushing sovereign yields to multi-year highs and increasing the opportunity cost of holding non-yielding assets such as platinum. Bearish sentiment was reinforced by the World Platinum Investment Council’s forecast of a market surplus in 2026, with total demand expected to decline 18%, including a 32% drop in Chinese jewelry demand and a 4% decline from automakers. Additional supply could also weigh on prices, as a state-owned firm in Zimbabwe, the world’s third-largest platinum reserves, plans to develop a mining project in Darwendale next year, with an estimated 44 million ounces of platinum-group metals. Over the longer term, however, rapid AI infrastructure expansion could support demand.

France Stocks Drop on Fiscal and Geopolitical Risks — The CAC 40 fell 1.2% to close at 7,769 on Wednesday, reversing the previous session’s gains and hitting its lowest level since March as geopolitical tensions and concerns over France’s fiscal outlook weighed on sentiment. Oil prices rose on renewed supply risks, raising inflationary pressures, while global yields resumed their rally. Meanwhile, ECB Governing Council member Emmanuel Moulin said France’s bond-market situation is serious but does not warrant intervention. Concerns over the government deficit persisted, weighing on credit-sensitive stocks. BNP Paribas shed 3.9% and Societe Generale lost 5%. Industrials also declined, with Safran down 3% and Schneider Electric losing 2.3%. On the economic front, France’s trade deficit narrowed to €6.1 billion in August from a revised €6.6 billion in July, beating expectations for a €6.5 billion shortfall. 【霍爾木茲海峽受阻致油運成本飆升至驚人新高】租用超大型油輪運輸原油的價格飆升至新高，為石油供應鏈增添了鉅額成本。根據倫敦波羅的海交易所週三的資料，租用一艘超大型油輪（VLCC）將美國原油運往亞洲的成本為7700萬美元。2025年的平均水平為920萬美元。 儘管原油期貨主力價格目前在每桶100美元左右交易，但這些期貨通常基於出口地的原油品級。對於200萬桶的貨物而言，運費意味著每桶額外增加約38.50美元的交付成本。在西非等部分市場，出口商被迫在出口地對貨物進行折價銷售。伊朗戰爭給油輪市場帶來了混亂，重塑了石油從中東運出的方式，並大幅增加了運輸時間。儘管該地區原油流量近幾周有所回升，但貿易流程遠比伊朗戰爭前複雜，導致交付時間延長，實際上大幅削減了船隊有效運力。

Supply disruptions tied to Iran tensions and the war in Ukraine have damaged refineries and tanker routes, pushing diesel to record highs and adding to inflationary pressure and costs for trucking and agriculture. The G7 last Friday agreed a 100 mln-barrel release; US president Trump warned he could ban US diesel exports if the G7 did not release more diesel. It remains unclear how much diesel versus crude would be released; IEA member states and the EU oil coordination group met Wednesday to discuss the plan.

Central Bank of Kenya Leaves Rates Steady — The central bank of Kenya left its key interest rate steady at 8.75% for a fourth consecutive meeting in October 2026, saying it saw inflation staying within its target range in the short term despite edging up in the past three months. Inflation in Kenya reached 6.8% in September 2026, its highest level since January 2024. Policymakers noted that government interventions, including subsidies and the temporary reduction of VAT on fuel, continue to mitigate inflationary pressures. The central bank also revised upwards its 2026 growth forecast to 5% from 4.9% seen in August, with El Niño representing a key risk to growth.

【日本東證股指成分股將大幅減少】日本東京證券交易所母公司日本交易所集團7日表示，東京證券交易所股票價格指數成分股將於10月底開始調整，調整後成分股數量將減少約40%至986只。此次調整的核心是成分股篩選標準發生變化。此前，原東證一部（現主要市場）上市企業基本都被納入東證股指。新規則下，上市企業能否進入東證股指，將取決於股票交易活躍度和流通股市值等指標，不再主要取決於企業在哪個市場上市。日本交易所集團表示，目前有683只股票不再符合納入條件，隨後在東證股指中的權重將逐步降低，到2028年7月降至零。同時，標準市場和增長市場中符合條件的股票也將被納入東證股指。這兩個市場此次共有35只股票入選，包括日本麥當勞控股公司、東映動畫、打車應用“GO”等。（新華社）

【New York Fed: U.S. one-year inflation expectations rise to 3.9%, highest in over three years】New York Fed September consumer survey: one-year inflation expectations rose to 3.9% from 3.6% in August, the highest since May 2023. Three-year expectations increased to 3.3% from 3.2%; five-year expectations remained at 3.0%. Households raised expected price gains for gasoline, food, rent, medical care and college. Respondents downgraded assessments of current and future finances and reported tighter perceived access to credit, though concern about inability to repay debt eased. Labor-market expectations improved: respondents saw a lower probability of higher unemployment in a year, reported less worry about involuntary job loss, and were more confident about finding a new job if displaced.

### 2026-10-07 22:50:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Albania #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Albania Leaves Key Rate Unchanged — The Bank of Albania kpet its benchmark rate unchanged at 2.5% at its October 2026 meeting, as policymakers stated that the current monetary stance remains appropriate to support inflation's return to target. Inflation averaged 3.3% in July-August, driven by higher fuel and unprocessed food prices, as global commodity costs rose amid heightened geopolitical tensions in the Middle East. Meanwhile, core and domestic inflation eased, although tight labor market conditions and rapid wage growth continued to pose upside risks over the medium-term. Economic activity remained solid, expanding 3.6% year-on-year in Q2, supported by consumption and investment. The central bank expects inflation to rise moderately over the next two quarters before gradually returning toward target over the medium term. Future policy decisions will depend on incoming inflation data, fiscal policy and exchange-rate developments.

【摩根士丹利再度建議做空歐元兌瑞郎，歐元區風險抬升】摩根士丹利策略師重新推薦做空歐元兌瑞郎，押注避險情緒升溫、歐元區資產風險溢價上行將拖累歐元。David Adams、Koichi Sugisaki、Andrew Watrous、Alexander Scholefield 在週三研報中寫道，做空歐元兌瑞郎可對沖 “財政與政治風險溢價進一步走高”，以及歐央行因債市波動而轉向更鴿派定價的風險。策略師表示：“儘管市場高度關注利差走闊，但歐元風險溢價的漲幅並不算大。”研報提到，瑞士央行對瑞郎走強的擔憂是該歐元兌瑞郎空頭交易的風險因素，有可能推高這一貨幣對。策略師建議做空歐元兌瑞郎，目標位 0.90，止損位 0.96。該行繼續推薦做空歐元兌瑞郎，預期歐元對多數貨幣將普遍走弱。策略師稱，歐元兌瑞郎大機率延續跌勢，但技術面與持倉情況顯示，短期可能進入盤整。

### 2026-10-07 22:12:01  #Trading Economics #Markets #Switzerland #Currency #Importance 0

Swiss Franc Weakens as Dollar Gains on Fed Outlook — The Swiss franc weakened to 0.83 per USD as investors favored the greenback amid expectations that the Federal Reserve will maintain a tighter policy stance, while the Swiss National Bank is widely expected to keep rates unchanged. Although weaker-than-expected US jobs data reduced expectations of a Fed hike this month, markets still anticipate another increase in December and at least one more in early 2027. Meanwhile, economists expect the SNB to keep its policy rate at 0% over the coming years, despite markets pricing in up to three hikes next year. Swiss inflation rose to 1.0% in September, driven by higher energy costs and reaching the midpoint of the SNB’s target range. However, safe-haven demand for the franc, amid higher oil prices and French fiscal concerns, is expected to limit imported inflation and keep core inflation subdued. The franc also benefits from Switzerland’s current-account and budget surpluses, a credible central bank, and a highly liquid currency market.

### 2026-10-07 22:08:52  #Trading Economics #Economy #Poland #Interest Rate #Importance 1

National Bank of Poland Holds Rate Steady — The National Bank of Poland left its reference policy rate unchanged at 3.75% at its October 2026 meeting, in line with expectations, marking the sixth consecutive meeting with rates at their lowest level since 2022. The decision came as government measures aimed at reducing fuel prices at the pump gave policymakers additional room to assess the inflation outlook. Still, headline inflation remains elevated, having accelerated to 4% in September due to higher fuel prices for private vehicles and reaching above the upper limit of the central bank's target of 2.5±1%. Policymakers highlighted uncertainty surrounding inflation and global activity, particularly the Middle East conflict, while other risks also include fiscal policy, energy price regulations, domestic activity and wage dynamics. The NBP also signaled it may intervene in the foreign exchange market.

Meanwhile, gold prices declined, weighing on miners, with Agnico Eagle, Barrick, WPM and Franco-Nevada falling around 3% each. In contrast, energy producers gained as the oil rally resumed, with Suncor, Imperial Oil and Cenovus up more than 1% each.

South African Rand Weakens as Dollar and Oil Prices Rise — The South African rand weakened to 16.70 per US dollar on Wednesday, its lowest level since late July, pressured by a stronger dollar and higher oil prices that weighed on risk-sensitive assets. The dollar remained supported by expectations of at least one more Fed rate hike this year, followed by further tightening in 2027. Meanwhile, Brent crude held above $100 a barrel amid continued Middle East supply risks and a strong hurricane forecast for US oil-producing regions. In South Africa, the central bank said that the risk of second-round inflation effects had become more pronounced as oil prices remained elevated for longer and El Niño clouded the outlook. The rand has also faced pressure from high petroleum imports, which have sustained dollar demand and increased the currency’s sensitivity to oil prices. South African policymakers raised interest rates by 25 basis points to 7.25% in September, with markets now pricing in two further increases over the next six months.

【美國參議員質疑貝森特美債回購操作 稱其干預行動“空前且混亂”】美國民主黨參議員伊麗莎白·沃倫就她所稱的美國國債市場“空前且混亂”的干預行動，向美國財政部長斯科特·貝森特提出了一系列問題。沃倫是參議院銀行委員會資深民主黨成員。她在10月7日致貝森特的一封信中詢問，財政部是否計劃縮減現金儲備，以為國債回購提供資金。她還追問財政部還在採取哪些措施，以壓低長期美國國債收益率，並要求貝森特在10月21日前作出答覆。貝森特此前擴大國債回購計劃，令市場參與者感到意外。該決定於8月19日宣佈，距離財政部公佈季度債務管理計劃僅兩週。沃倫表示，政府近期的做法正在削弱財政部長期以來在發債策略上堅持的“定期且可預測”原則。在長期借貸成本升至21世紀初以來最高水平後，貝森特採取了上述行動。這一輪收益率上升很大程度上由伊朗戰爭引發的通脹壓力推動。儘管財政部擴大了回購操作，基準10年期美國國債收益率仍繼續攀升，本週觸及2002年以來最高水平。沃倫還詢問貝森特，財政部是否評估過更高國債收益率對汽車貸款、住房貸款等面向消費者的借貸利率有何影響。她同時追問，財政部是否會動用財政部一般賬戶(TGA)資金，以擴大國債回購規模。

### 2026-10-07 21:29:56  #市場

【西班牙工會號召舉行大罷工 反對黨稱此舉旨在服務桑切斯政治議程】西班牙最大的幾個工會準備號召於11月11日舉行大罷工，以聚焦住房問題。屆時，定於11月29日舉行的提前大選即將拉開競選序幕。此前，由反對黨主導的議會否決了一項旨在提高住房可負擔性的法案，工會此次行動正是對此作出的回應。該法案遭否決後，社會黨籍首相佩德羅·桑切斯宣佈舉行提前大選。司法部長Félix Bolaños週三接受採訪時表示，“我認同這次罷工的訴求。西班牙社會普遍認為住房市場執行不正常。右翼和極右翼又採取了什麼措施？什麼都沒有。”反對黨則稱，這些抗議活動是動員選民、影響選舉的一項策略。人民黨發言人Jaime de los Santos表示，“過去有太多理由可以為了捍衛勞動者權利而舉行示威，但他們並沒有這麼做。這顯然是一項政治決定，目的是服務於佩德羅·桑切斯的政治議程。”工會將在週三晚些時候透過投票正式確認罷工日期。工會要求政府幹預住房市場以遏制價格上漲，同時要求提高工資，因為薪資漲幅遠遠落後於住房成本的上漲。

### 2026-10-07 21:14:36  #公司 #國際

【敘利亞有望成為伊拉克原油繞過霍爾木茲海峽的替代出口路線】目前，大批卡車正排隊等候，準備將原油經陸路運至地中海沿岸的港口。敘利亞有望成為伊拉克原油繞過霍爾木茲海峽的替代出口路線。目前，大批卡車正排隊等候，準備將原油經陸路運至地中海沿岸的港口。敘利亞國有石油公司執行長優素福·基布拉維在接受採訪時表示，伊拉克政府已請求敘利亞協助出口原油，這將在現有的燃料油運輸量基礎上進一步增加。基布拉維表示：“我預計透過卡車經敘利亞出口伊拉克原油的工作將盡快啟動，因為兩國政府之間的協議已接近達成。因此，希望到10月中旬，首批滿載原油的卡車車隊就能抵達敘利亞。”目前，伊拉克已在使用一支卡車車隊將其部分燃料油運至敘利亞地中海沿岸的巴尼亞斯港，貨物在此裝上油輪後繼續出口。在霍爾木茲海峽關閉對其財政造成重創後，巴格達正試圖鞏固其石油收入。除敘利亞外，伊拉克還透過約旦運送滿載燃料油的卡車。交易商稱，少量原油——也是伊拉克經濟的支柱——也以同樣的方式透過卡車運輸。

### 2026-10-07 21:11:44  #公司 #市場

【國際貨幣基金組織預計高油價將持續至明年】國際貨幣基金組織（IMF）總裁克里斯塔利娜·格奧爾基耶娃7日在新加坡表示，即使當前海灣地區的戰事很快結束，高能源價格仍可能持續一段時間。格奧爾基耶娃當天在國際貨幣基金組織和世界銀行集團2026年秋季年會預熱活動上致辭時說，從目前布倫特原油期貨價格走勢看，預計高油價將持續至2027年。她說：“到目前為止，能源價格衝擊雖然規模較大，但總體仍在可控範圍內。這主要得益於能源利用效率、燃料來源多元化、儲備釋放、供應鏈靈活性，以及市場正常的供需調節。”她指出，儘管海灣地區能源供應正在緩慢恢復，但受高昂運輸成本等因素影響，國際油價目前仍維持在每桶約100美元的水平。此外，受全球煉油產能不足影響，柴油等成品油零售價格處於歷史高位。格奧爾基耶娃說，海灣地區天然氣供應也仍受到嚴重影響，尤其是液化天然氣運輸渠道受限。只要經由霍爾木茲海峽的航運仍面臨威脅，這種影響就可能持續。“隨著各國補充能源儲備，以及北半球寒冷季節臨近、能源需求上升，能源價格上漲壓力可能進一步加大。”她說。（新華社）

### 2026-10-07 21:11:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Used Car Prices MoM #Importance 1

US Used Car Prices Down for 3rd Month — The Manheim Used Vehicle Value Index, a widely followed gauge of wholesale used-vehicle prices in the US, fell 1.1% mom in September 2026, extending the decline for a third consecutive month. Non-EV prices were down 1.7% while EV prices edged up 0.6%. "We are in the weakest season for wholesale valuations, and as September closed, depreciation was steeper than we typically see this time of year. The first half of the year actually showed more appreciation than usual, even in the face of higher fuel prices. But with the conflict in the Middle East ongoing, diesel prices at record highs, and interest rates climbing rapidly - increasingly worrying both businesses and consumers - wholesale prices have felt the sting" according to Jeremy Robb, Chief Economist, Cox Automotive. Year-on-year, used-vehicle prices fell 0.6%, the first decline since March 2025. Strength among EVs and Compact Cars was more than offset by Midsize Cars, Pickups and SUVs that continue to trend down.

【微特稿日本頻發企業客戶資料安全事件】日本多家企業近日接連報告使用者資料安全事件。日本大型旅行社H.I.S.集團7日說，旗下泰國子公司伺服器遭入侵，或導致數百名客戶護照資訊外洩。前一天，西鐵城鐘錶公司等企業也報告伺服器遭入侵，客戶資訊可能洩露。據共同社報道，H.I.S.集團的泰國子公司2025年12月11日收到對伺服器進行未經授權訪問的警報，隨即切斷了相關伺服器與網路的連線並限制訪問。後續調查發現，627名客戶的護照資訊可能外洩，包括姓名、性別、生日、護照號和有效期、過敏資訊。H.I.S.集團就該事件表示道歉，稱先前沒有立即對外公開是因為需要“耗費長時間正確分析和核實”伺服器中儲存的大量資料。網際網路集團GMO旗下子公司GMO研究與人工智慧公司6日說，其調查問卷網站infoQ遭入侵，94.85萬條會員記錄被盜，涉及會員姓名、電郵、住址、電話和密碼。該公司還說，總額290萬日元（約合12.29萬元人民幣）的會員積分獎勵遭擅自兌換為亞馬遜禮品卡程式碼。日本折扣零售企業MrMax公司6日說，企業應用程式和網路商店使用的伺服器客戶資訊外洩，可能影響170萬名客戶，外洩資訊包括姓名、電郵和電話號碼。西鐵城鐘錶公司同日說，由一家簽約公司運營的伺服器遭非法訪問，大約10萬名客戶的個人資訊可能洩露。日本內閣官房長官木原稔在記者會上表示，政府非常重視這一系列事件，將迅速收集資訊以評估損失情況。（新華社）

Arabica Coffee Futures Falls From 1-Month High — Arabica coffee futures fell below $2.95 per pound, after reaching its highest level since early September, as expectations of ample supply from Brazil helped ease concerns over next year's crops. The 2026 harvest from the world's top coffee producer, Brazil, is seen at record numbers, with supply estimated at 67.6 million processed bags, up 19.6% from the previous year. Additionally, exports from Brazil in September jumped to its highest level of shippping volume in 20 months, reinforcing expectations of strong output. Meanwhile, Hedgepoint Global Markets estimates a global surplus of 8.9 million bags for the current crop year. Still, concerns over the El Niño phenomenon continue to underpin prices. With rains returning to most producing regions, growers are entering the fertilizer application period for the 2027/28 crop, although producers currently have reduced purchasing power over the input.

Sugar Prices Near 20-Month Highs — US sugar futures were above 20 cents per pound, their highest level since February 2025, as unfavorable weather conditions in major producing countries raised concerns about global production. In Brazil, rains have delayed cane crushing in the Centre-South region and are expected to reduce sugar output this season. A significant portion of the sugarcane crop could be left to be crushed during the next harvest season, potentially extending the current crushing season into late February or early March. The El Niño weather event is also expected to weigh on production. In India, a weak monsoon could curb yields, while scorching summer temperatures in Europe are expected to result in the worst sugar-beet harvest in more than a decade. In Thailand, drought conditions in key sugar-producing regions are also threatening production. Sugar prices have risen more than 8% so far in October and are up around 40% since July.

Chile Trade Surplus Widens in September — Chile's trade surplus widened to $2.05 billion in September 2026 from $1.76 billion a year earlier, the largest surplus since June. Exports rose 14.1% to $10.58 billion, driven by higher mining shipments (12.2%), particularly copper (14%) and lithium carbonate (127.3%), as well as industrial goods (19.1%), notably food products (18.8%), basic metals (42.7%) and metal products, machinery and equipment (32.6%). Sales of agricultural, forestry and fishing products also increased 8.9%. Meanwhile, imports advanced 13.5% to $8.54 billion, supported by higher purchases of consumer goods (13.1%), mainly cellphones (35.4%), and intermediate goods (25.8%), particularly energy products such as diesel (182.1% vs. 63.2% in August) and oil (2.1% vs. 120.5%). In contrast, purchases of capital goods declined 9.4%.

### 2026-10-07 19:13:39  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Mortgage Applications #Importance 1

US Mortgage Applications Fall to 20-Month Low — The volume of mortgage applications in the US fell by 4.2% in the week ending October 2, marking the fifth consecutive decline and reaching its lowest level since January 2025, according to data from the Mortgage Bankers Association. The drop tracked a sharp increase in mortgage rates over the period, as the benchmark 30-year fixed rate averaged a near-three-year high of 7.49%. Yields on long-term securities continued to surge at the turn of the month despite Treasury efforts to tame the long end by increasing bill issuance and expanding buybacks of nominal notes and bonds. Applications to refinance a loan, which are sensitive to short-term changes in interest rates, fell by 7.5% from the previous week, while purchase applications declined by 2.1%.

### 2026-10-07 19:11:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Mortgage Rate #Importance 2

US 30-Year Mortgage Rates Hit 7.49% — The average 30-year fixed mortgage rate jumped another 19 basis points to 7.49% in the week ended October 2, following an 18-basis-point increase the previous week and reaching its highest level since late November 2023, according to the Mortgage Bankers Association. It marked a seventh straight weekly increase, bringing the cumulative rise to 140 basis points since US-Israeli strikes against Iran began in late February. The latest increase followed another rise in Treasury yields, with both 10-year and 30-year rates reaching their highest levels since 2002 amid persistent energy-driven inflation and expectations for at least one more Fed hike this year, followed by further tightening in 2027. Total mortgage applications fell 4.2%, extending their decline to a fifth consecutive week. Refinancing applications dropped 7.5%, while purchase applications fell 2.1%.

### 2026-10-07 18:43:59  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Stock Market #Importance 1

Indian Stocks Pull Back after RBI Hike — Indian shares closed firmly lower on Wednesday with broad pressure from most sectors after the Reserve Bank of India raised its benchmark policy rate by 25 bps, as expected. The S&P/BSE Sensex fell 0.6% to 72,639 and the NSE Nifty 50 dropped 0.8% to 22,603. It was the RBI's first rate increase in over three years, warranted by a rebound in the outlook for inflation and mounting pressure on the rupee driven by capital outflows and surging energy import costs. The increase in borrowing costs is likely to weigh on credit demand and impact earnings, with index heavyweights Titan, Asian Paints, Larsen & Toubro, Tata Steel, and Adani Ports falling between 2% and 4%. Tech also fell following a pullback in US equity markets, with Infosys and HCL Tech down around 2% each. On the other hand, state and private banks outperformed, closing near the flatline in aggregate.

### 2026-10-07 18:28:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #Luxembourg #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Luxembourg Inflation Rate at 5-Month High — The annual inflation rate in Luxembourg rose to 2.9% in September 2026 from 2.2% in August, marking the highest level since April. Price growth accelerated in several categories, including housing and utilities (1.3% vs 0.5%), transport (7.3% vs 5.3%), food and non-alcoholic beverages (1.5% vs 1.4%), clothing and footwear (1.1% vs 1.0%), recreation and culture (3.4% vs 1.5%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (2.6% vs 2.0%), information and communication (3.1% vs 0.9%), and personal care, social protection, and miscellaneous goods and services (4.2% vs 2.1%). At the same time, inflation held steady for restaurants and hotels at 3.2%, as well as furnishings, household equipment, and routine household maintenance at 1.5%. Meanwhile, inflation eased for health (1.7% vs 1.8%) and education (0.6% vs 6.2%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices eased 0.6% in September from a 1.2% increase in August.

Italy BTP Yield Rises Amid Debt Concerns — Italy's 10-Year BTP yield climbed above 4.62%, remaining near it highest level in three years, as concerns over Europe's highly indebted countries continue to pressure the bond market, with France's fiscal position remaining the key concern. In Italy, Premier Meloni seeks to lower the nation's 2026 budget deficit below the EU's ceiling of 3%, as defense spending was cut to €14 billion from the €22 billion previously planned. However, the government is expecting the deficit to widen again in the following two years, due to higher energy and defense spending, while its debt-to-GDP ratio, which is currrently at 138.6%, is expected overtake Greece's this year, leaving Italy as the euro zone's most indebted country. Both France and Italy are to hold elections next year, while a Spanish snap election in November adds to political uncertainty across Europe. Elevated energy prices also remain in focus, keeping inflation high, while market's are pricing in roughly two ECB hikes by March 2027.

【BofA: Bonds first time in decades competitive; S&P 10-year returns may trail Treasuries】BofA head of US equities and quant strategy Savita Subramanian said bonds are, for the first time in decades, a genuinely competitive alternative to equities. With the US 10-year yield above 5%, BofA’s valuation model indicates the S&P 500’s annualized return over the next 10 years may be lower than current Treasury yields. She warned high investor sentiment raises equity downside risk and limits further upside. Policymakers, including the Fed and the US Treasury, are monitoring the long end to avoid too-large increases in long-term rates; demographics and potential AI-driven disinflation imply a lower long-term ceiling for US rates than in the 1970s–80s, making sustained Treasury yields above 6–7% unlikely and improving the case for bond allocations.

### 2026-10-07 17:40:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mauritius #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Mauritius Inflation Rate Reaches Highest in Over 2 Years — The annual inflation rate in Mauritius accelerated sharply to 5.7% in September 2026, the highest level since February 2024, up from 4.9% in August. It marked the third consecutive month of rising inflation, driven mainly by a sharp increase in food price growth, which accelerated to 6.2% from 3.9%, and transport, which rose to 7.6% from 6%. Additional upward pressure came from restaurants and hotels (9% vs 8.6%), furnishings (4.6% vs 4.4%), clothing and footwear (2.5% vs 2.4%), and education (6.3% vs 5.4%). Meanwhile, inflation remained elevated for housing and utilities at 8.9%, while recreation, sport and culture stood at 2.7%. From the previous month, consumer prices rose 0.6% following a 0.4% rise in August.

Sterling Slips as Oil Rebound Revives Inflation Concerns — Sterling traded around $1.32, close to a three-month low against the dollar touched last week, as a rebound in oil prices added to inflation concerns and reinforced expectations for UK interest rates to remain higher for longer. Brent crude rose back above $100 a barrel as Iran intensified attacks on shipping in the Strait of Hormuz, prompting markets to price more than 100 basis points of Bank of England tightening by the end of next year. BoE policymaker Catherine Mann said Tuesday that inflation above the Bank’s 2% target appeared embedded in the economy and warned it could reach 4% around the turn of the year, when wage negotiations typically take place. Meanwhile, the dollar remained supported by expectations that the Federal Reserve will raise rates for a second time this year in December. Against the euro, sterling rose to its highest level since June last year, as concerns over France’s deepening fiscal crisis weighed on the common currency.

### 2026-10-07 17:27:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #Luxembourg #GDP Growth Rate #Importance 1

Luxembourg Economy Contracts in Q2 — Luxembourg’s economy contracted 0.9% quarter-on-quarter in the second quarter of 2026, reversing from a revised 0.3% growth in the previous three-month period. This marked the first contraction after six straight quarters of growth, as declines were recorded in gross fixed investment (-1.9% vs 6.3% in Q1), and household expenditure (-0.3% vs -0.8%), while growth eased for government spending (1.1% vs 1.8%). Net trade also weighed on the economy, with both exports (-0.4% vs -1.2%) and imports (-0.8% vs -2.1%) declining at softer rates. On the production side, declines were seen in industry, including energy and water distribution (-3.7% vs -1.6%), construction (-2.1% vs 2.7%), trade, transport, accommodation and food services (-0.8% vs 1.1%), and financial and insurance activities (-1.5% vs -0.7%). Conversely, growth was recorded in agriculture, forestry, and fishing (4.5% vs 0.9%). On an annual basis, GDP grew 1.3% in Q2, slowing from a revised 2.8% rise in the prior quarter.

【ING questions feasibility of National Rally fiscal plan, calls growth assumptions too optimistic】ING economist Charlotte de Montpellier says the fiscal plan from Marine Le Pen’s National Rally looks difficult to implement. The party, which leads first‑round polls, pledges annual spending cuts of about €136bn to push France’s public spending below 50% of GDP by 2032. ING says some savings are feasible but the largest cuts lack implementation detail, and the macro assumptions—GDP growth of 0.9% in 2026 and 2027 and 1.8% in 2028—are unrealistic. ING also flags a key inconsistency: the plan combines large-scale consolidation with around €69.3bn of tax cuts and new spending scheduled for 2027, making the stated fiscal targets hard to achieve.

### 2026-10-07 17:14:34  #Trading Economics #Economy #Iceland #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Iceland Trade Gap Widens in September — Iceland’s trade deficit widened to ISK 45.7 billion in September 2026 from ISK 44.9 billion in the same month a year earlier. Imports rose by 9% to ISK 133.2 billion, driven largely by higher purchases of fuels and lubricants (+79%), capital goods (+13%), and consumer goods (+9%), while imports declined in food and beverages (-11%), industrial supplies (-3%), and transport equipment (-13%). Meanwhile exports increased by 13% to ISK 87.5 billion, lifted by increased shipments of farmed fish (+64%), manufacturing products (+29%), which offset the declines in marine products (-7%), agriculture products (-7%), and other products (-29%). Over the last twelve months, Iceland’s trade deficit totaled ISK 470.7 billion, narrowing from ISK 521.4 billion a year earlier. Imports fell by 2% to ISK 1,435.6 billion, while exports grew by 2% to ISK 964.9 billion.

Singapore Forex Reserves Hit Highest Level Since 2022 — Singapore’s official foreign exchange reserves rose to SGD 553.57 billion in September 2026, the highest level since February 2022, from SGD 550.73 billion in August. The increase was mainly driven by higher gold and foreign exchange reserves, which climbed to SGD 543.62 billion from SGD 540.73 billion. Meanwhile, special drawing rights edged down to SGD 8.18 billion from SGD 8.21 billion, while the reserve position in the IMF declined slightly to SGD 1.77 billion from SGD 1.78 billion. Compared with a year earlier, official foreign exchange reserves were significantly higher, rising from SGD 506.83 billion in September 2025.

### 2026-10-07 17:08:45  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #COALINDIA.NS #Impact bullish #NTPC.NS #Impact bearish #POWERGRID.NS #Impact mixed

【India coal-fired power stocks near five-year low; over 40% of capacity has coal for four days or less】As of Oct 4, media reported India’s coal-fired power plant inventories were near a five-year low: about 69 plants, representing over 40% of national coal-fired capacity, had coal stocks sufficient for four days or less. Unseasonably high October temperatures and patchy rainfall have kept cooling, irrigation and industrial electricity demand elevated. Coal deliveries are being disrupted by heavy rains that have halted some mines and by persistent rail-transport bottlenecks, forcing plants to draw down on-site inventories. The low stockpiles raise the risk of widespread outages.

### 2026-10-07 16:45:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #Iceland #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Central Bank of Iceland Leaves Key Rate Unchanged at 8% — The Central Bank of Iceland left its key policy rate unchanged at 8% in October 2026, pausing its monetary tightening after three consecutive 25bp hikes. Headline inflation continued to accelerate following the previous meeting, as antecipated, reaching a two-year high of 5.9% in September, driven mainly by the impact of the conflict in the Middle East and higher public levies. Underlying inflation, however, has held relatively flat, at just over 4%. Policymakers also noted that economic activity has begun to slow, contracting 1.1% in Q2 after expanding 2.8% in the previous quarter. Nevertheless, the central bank expects inflation to decline relatively rapidly in 2027, although uncertainty remains high around global economic conditions and the domestic labor market. Future rate decisions will continue to depend on developments in economic activity, inflation and inflation expectations, which remain elevated.

【恒生科技指數誕生6年編纂規則首次大規模調整：取消行業要求 成分股數量由30只擴至50只】恒生科技指數誕生近6年來首次進行大規模調整。成分股數量將由30只增加至50只，恒指公司將引入“雙軌”選股機制，以“市值”和“收入增長”兩項為準則，恒指公司表示，增長快的科技公司未必是市值最大的公司，40只成分股按市值規模納入，10只按過去12個月收入增速排名納入。此外，科指將取消行業要求。即各行業有一定科技濃度的公司均有機會被納入科指。恒指公司表示，各行業均有具備科技屬性的公司，科技發展已經跨越傳統行業限制。科指將有六大主題，分別為數碼平臺及解決方案、人工智慧、先進硬體、機器人和自動化，雲端、以及前沿科技。當中前沿科技為新增主題。科指11月20日公佈檢討結果，修訂將在今年12月7日生效。

### 2026-10-07 16:41:11  #Trading Economics #Economy #Taiwan #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Taiwan Inflation Hits 31-Month High — Taiwan’s annual inflation rate rose to 2.7% in September 2026 from 2.05% in the previous month. It marked the highest level since February 2024 and the fifth consecutive month above the central bank’s 2% target, as food prices accelerated sharply, advancing to a one-year high of 2.64% from 0.77% in August. Inflation also climbed for transportation and communication (4.07% vs 2.79%), as well as clothing (2.32% vs 2.09%), housing (2.38% vs 2.31%), and healthcare (0.70% vs 0.57%). Meanwhile, price growth moderated slightly in education and entertainment (3.15% vs 3.22%), and miscellaneous goods and services (2.77% vs 2.81%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, consumer prices eased to 0.13% from 0.19% in August, marking the lowest reading since November 2025.

### 2026-10-07 16:32:50  #Trading Economics #Markets #Euro Area #Currency #Importance 1

Euro Falls Toward 17-Month Low — The euro weakened below $1.12, moving closer to a 17-month low of $1.116 touched earlier this week, as oil prices rebounded amid renewed Middle East tensions and fiscal concerns continued to weigh on sentiment. Brent crude rose back above $100 a barrel as Iran intensified attacks on shipping in the Strait of Hormuz, reinforcing expectations of further interest-rate hikes from major central banks. Markets now price roughly 75 basis points of ECB and 85 basis points of Fed hikes by the end of next year. Meanwhile, French bonds remain under pressure as political uncertainty ahead of the 2027 election raises doubts over the country’s ability to repair its finances. The minority government has unveiled a plan to cut the budget deficit, although the fiscal watchdog warned that its economic assumptions were “optimistic.” Political uncertainty is also rising elsewhere, with Spain calling a snap election for November 29 and Italy heading to the polls next year.

### 2026-10-07 16:16:12  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Foreign Exchange Reserves #Importance 1

Czech Republic Forex Reserves at 6-Month Low — Foreign exchange reserves in the Czech Republic fell to USD 180.1 billion in September 2026 from a record high of USD 184.6 billion in the previous month. It marked the smallest figure since March, mainly driven by a decline in foreign currency reserves to USD 162.8 billion from USD 166.8 billion in August. The IMF reserve position also fell to USD 0.767 billion from USD 0.775 billion, while SDRs declined to USD 3.50 billion from USD 3.54 billion and gold reserves dropped to USD 11.66 billion from USD 12.63 billion. On the other hand, other reserve assets increased to USD 1.35 billion from USD 0.80 billion.

【澤連斯基：俄軍襲擊造成烏多地人員傷亡 烏打擊俄石油設施】當地時間10月7日，烏克蘭總統澤連斯基在社交平臺通報，俄軍當天凌晨對基輔及烏克蘭12個州的基礎設施發動襲擊，能源設施是此次襲擊的主要目標之一。切爾尼戈夫州一棟五層住宅樓遭襲。截至目前，已有5人死亡、26人受傷，其中包括5名兒童，救援人員正在持續搜救。澤連斯基表示，俄軍襲擊還造成5棟公寓樓與4棟高層住宅樓受損。克列緬丘格市有2人死亡、8人受傷。扎波羅熱市一棟高層住宅樓遭無人機襲擊並受損，基輔市遭襲致2人死亡，基洛沃格勒州另有2人死亡。此外，他表示，俄軍此次共發射70枚導彈，其中包括大量彈道導彈，並使用無人機，烏方僅攔截其中一部分。他說，俄羅斯彈道導彈仍是烏克蘭面臨的最大挑戰，烏方目前急需“愛國者”防空系統攔截彈。澤連斯基還稱，過去一天，烏軍對俄羅斯4個目標實施打擊，以回應俄羅斯針對烏克蘭平民發動的襲擊。澤連斯基稱，遭打擊目標包括位於俄羅斯彼爾姆州、薩馬拉州和阿斯特拉罕州的兩個石油設施和一個訓練場。此外，烏方也在黑海採取了行動。（央視新聞）

### 2026-10-07 16:12:20  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bulgaria #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Bulgaria Retail Sales Growth Accelerates in August — Retail sales in Bulgaria rose by 5.9% year-on-year in August 2026, following a six-month low of 5.6% in the previous month. Activity increased in automotive fuels and lubricants (8.3% vs 6.6% in July) and non-food goods, except automotive fuels and lubricants (4.2% vs 4.1%), driven largely by higher sales in computer and communication equipment and other consumer goods (4.6% vs 1.9%), and mail order, telephone or internet (28.1% vs 22.5%). Moreover, sales growth remained steady in food, beverages, and tobacco (at 7.5%). On a monthly basis, retail activity grew by 0.3% in August, rebounding from a downwardly revised 0.3% drop in the preceding period.

### 2026-10-07 16:06:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Foreign Exchange Reserves #Importance 1

India’s FX Reserves Fall for Fourth Week — India’s foreign exchange reserves fell for a fourth consecutive week to $734.6 billion as of October 2, 2026, according to a footnote to Reserve Bank of India Governor Sanjay Malhotra’s monetary policy statement. Reserves have declined from their recent peak of $785.71 billion on September 4, a drop of around $50 billion in less than a month. The drawdown reflects RBI intervention in the foreign-exchange market, including dollar sales to smooth the rupee’s decline and sell/buy FX swaps aimed at absorbing surplus liquidity. Despite the central bank’s support, including its first rate hike since 2023 in October, the rupee has continued to weaken, pressured by elevated oil prices, rising US Treasury yields and increased foreign equity outflows.

DAX Snaps 3-day Gain — Frankfurt’s DAX 40 fell 0.8% to around 25,240 in early trading on Wednesday, snapping a three-session winning streak, as a renewed rise in oil prices and elevated bond yields weighed on sentiment. Brent crude rose back above $100 a barrel as Iran stepped up attacks on tankers transiting the Strait of Hormuz, fueling inflation concerns and raising the prospect of higher interest rates. Investors now await the Federal Reserve’s meeting minutes later in the day for clues on the path of US interest rates. Meanwhile, better-than-expected German industrial production provided some support, with output rising 2.0% month-on-month in August, well above expectations of a 0.5% increase, keeping hopes of an emerging economic recovery alive. On the corporate front, shares of Infineon fell 3.6%, while Siemens Energy declined 2.0%, Deutsche Bank dropped 1.3%, and Vonovia lost 1.3%.

French Stocks Hit Over 6-Month Low — The CAC 40 fell 0.7% to 7,808 on Wednesday, reversing the previous session’s gains and hitting its lowest level since March, as geopolitical tensions and concerns over France’s fiscal outlook weighed on sentiment. Oil prices rose as concerns over further Houthi attacks on Saudi Arabia outweighed relief from signs of recovering Middle Eastern oil supplies. In France, as investors doubt whether the government can cut the deficit to 5.0% of GDP in 2027, ECB Governing Council member Emmanuel Moulin said the country’s bond-market situation is serious but does not warrant intervention. On the economic front, France’s trade deficit narrowed to €6.1 billion in August from a revised €6.6 billion in July, beating expectations for a €6.5 billion shortfall. Exports fell 0.8% mom to €54.2 billion, while imports declined 1.5% to €60.3 billion. Most stocks traded lower, led by financials, with BNP Paribas (-1.2%), Crédit Agricole (-1.1%), and Société Générale (-1.6%) among the biggest drags.

### 2026-10-07 15:35:46  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Czech Trade Deficit Widens in August — The trade deficit in the Czech Republic widened to CZK 5.3 billion in August 2026 from CZK 0.3 billion surplus a year earlier, exceeding market expectations of CZK 4.4 billion. Imports rose 10.2% year-on-year to CZK 377.2 billion, driven mainly by higher purchases of crude petroleum and natural gas (+67.6%), coke and refined petroleum products (+47.9%), other transport equipment (+81.4%), and computers, electronic and optical products (+21.3%). Meanwhile, exports rose 8.5% year-on-year to CZK 371.9 billion driven largely by stronger sales of crude petroleum and natural gas (+871.0%), electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning (+100.3%), other transport equipment (+32.7%), and computers, electronic and optical products (+20.6%). In the January–August period, the trade surplus reached CZK 83.7 billion, down by CZK 42.8 billion a year earlier, as imports outpaced exports, rising 5.1% and 3.6%, respectively.

European Stocks Lower — European stocks fell on Wednesday, with the STOXX 50 down 0.6% and the STOXX 600 losing 0.4%, snapping a three-day winning streak. The decline came as renewed gains in oil prices, driven by concerns over further Houthi attacks on Saudi Arabia, outweighed relief from signs of recovering Middle Eastern oil supplies. The rise in crude prices also pushed government bond yields higher, as investors remained concerned about the inflationary impact of elevated energy costs. Mining and technology stocks led the declines, with Infineon Technologies falling 2.8%, ASML Holding down 1.4% and Glencore losing 1%. Banks also came under pressure, with HSBC declining 1.5%, Banco Santander 0.9%, BBVA 1.1% and UniCredit 1.2%. Industrial stocks weakened as well, with Siemens down 1.8% and Siemens Energy falling 1.7%. On the other hand, the auto sector was a bright spot, with Renault (1.8%), Stellantis (1.7%) and Volkswagen (1.8%) posting gains.

### 2026-10-07 15:25:01  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

India 10Y Yield Hits 2-Year High as RBI Tightens Policy — The yield on India’s 10-year G-Sec rose to around 7.23%, hitting more than two-year highs as investors weighed the RBI’s rate hike against persistent inflation risks, higher global bond yields, and elevated oil prices. The benchmark 6.94% 2036 bond yield gained 2 basis points, while the 10-year government bond yield later climbed 5 basis points after the RBI raised its repo rate by 25 basis points to 5.50%, the highest in a year and its first hike since February 2023. The move was in line with expectations and came as the central bank shifted its stance to “calibrated tightening,” with retail inflation reaching 4.8% in August after rising for 10 straight months. Markets are now watching for further liquidity tightening through additional debt sales or a modest increase in the cash reserve ratio. In September, the RBI sold INR 1 trillion ($10.39 billion) of bonds, the largest in at least a decade.

Czechia Construction Output Falls 1.4% in August — Construction output in the Czech Republic fell 1.4% year-on-year in August 2026, reversing a 1.8% increase in the previous month. Building construction rose 5.9% year-on-year, accelerating from 4.9% growth in July, while civil engineering declined further, falling 12.3% after a 3.4% drop. A total of 5,684 building permits were granted, up 1.4% from a year earlier, while the floor area of newly permitted buildings increased 20.2% year-on-year. Meanwhile, construction started on 3,922 dwellings, climbed 42.3% year-on-year, driven by increases in both family houses and multi-dwelling buildings. Meanwhile, 2,852 dwellings were completed, down 3.0% year-on-year. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, construction output fell 0.1% in August, reversing a 0.4% increase in July.

Seychelles Inflation Highest Since 2024 — The annual inflation rate in Seychelles climbed to 1.22% in September 2026 from 0.93% in the previous month, marking the highest reading since December 2024. Inflation accelerated across all major groups, with fish prices rising 6.07% year-on-year (2.83% in August), while non-food and other food prices increased to 1.28% and 0.32%, respectively, from 1% and 0.28% in the previous month. Within non-food items, prices rose fastest for alcoholic beverages (5.25% vs 4.53%), transport (4.84% vs 3.46%), restaurants and hotels (3.43% vs 2.84%), and education (2.49% vs 2.21%). Other food prices were driven by vegetables (4.01% vs 3.85%), non-alcoholic beverages (2.77% vs 2.19%), and meat (1.98% vs 1.51%), while oils and fats eased to 3.79% from 4.65%. Meanwhile, annual core inflation rose to 1.24% from 0.97% in August. On a monthly basis, consumer prices increased by 0.24%, up from 0.18% in August.

France Current Account Deficit Smallest in 4 Months — France’s current account deficit narrowed to a seasonally adjusted €1.5 billion in August 2026 from a downwardly revised €3.9 billion in the previous month. Czech Industrial Output at 3-Month Low — Industrial production in the Czech Republic increased 2.6% year-on-year in August 2026, below market expectations and the previous month's 3.1% growth. Meanwhile, manufacturing activity edged up to 2.7% from 2.6%, supported by stronger production of wood and wood products (4.4% vs 2.0%) and paper and paper products (8.1% vs 0.6%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial output rose 0.3% in August, rebounding from a 1.1% decline in the previous month.

【德法尋求進一步放寬歐盟燃油車禁令】據報道，3名知情人士表示，德國總理默茨和法國總統馬克龍就汽車產業達成協議：法國在燃油車淘汰問題上向德國做出讓步，作為交換，德國則在“購買歐洲產品”的要求上做出妥協。根據現行歐盟法規，到2035年，新註冊車輛的平均二氧化碳排放量必須比2021年水平降低100%，意味著車隊排放上限為每公里0克二氧化碳，實際上等同於禁止銷售燃油車。歐盟委員會此前在德國施壓下，於2025年12月提議放寬這一禁令。根據歐盟委員會的方案，到2035年汽車二氧化碳排放量必須下降90%。剩餘10%的排放額度，汽車製造商可以透過使用低碳歐洲鋼材以及電子燃料和生物燃料進行抵消。默茨和馬克龍的計劃將進一步超越歐盟委員會的提案。歐盟外交官表示，雙方已經就大致框架達成一致，但具體細節仍在談判中。

France Trade Gap Narrows More Than Expected — France’s trade deficit narrowed to €6.1 billion in August 2026 from a revised €6.6 billion in July, coming below the expected €6.5 billion shortfall. Exports fell 0.8% month-on-month to €54.2 billion, as shipments of transport equipment (-5.7%), agricultural, forestry and fishing products (-5.4%), and other industrial products (-0.4%) declined. This was partly offset by higher refined petroleum product exports (+10.5%) and mechanical, electrical, electronic and computer equipment (+2.6%). By destination, exports declined to Asia (-8.4%), the Middle East (-43.2%), and Africa (-6.3%), while increasing to the EU (+0.9%) and the Americas (+9.5%). Meanwhile, imports fell 1.5% to €60.3 billion, led by transport equipment (-3.9%), refined petroleum products and coke (-3.4%), and agricultural, forestry and fishing products (-1.8%). By origin, imports decreased from the EU (-1.2%), Asia (-0.5%), and the Middle East (-39.6%), while increasing from Africa (+6.5%) and the Americas (+1.3%).

Coal Hits Three-Year High — Coal futures climbed above $150 per ton, reaching their highest level since September 2023 after Australia’s High Court rejected an appeal by Mach Energy Australia over the planned expansion of its coal mine in NSW. The court found that planning authorities had failed to adequately account for emissions generated by customers who burn the mine’s coal. Minerals Council of Australia chief executive Tania Constable said the ruling could significantly undermine investment and send a negative signal to Australia’s trading and investment partners about sovereign risk, potentially putting future supply under pressure. Meanwhile, global coal demand is expected to reach a record this year as gas supplies face disruptions linked to the Iran war. The IEA said in its Coal Mid-Year Update 2026 that higher LNG prices caused by the Strait of Hormuz blockade are encouraging economies such as China, India, Japan, South Korea and parts of Europe to rely more heavily on coal-fired power generation.

German Industrial Output Rises Most in 17 Months — Germany’s industrial production rose 2.0% month-on-month in August 2026, exceeding market forecasts for a 0.5% increase and rebounding from a revised 1.2% drop in July. This marked the strongest growth since March 2025, mainly driven by a 9.3% advance in the construction sector, with increases in specialized construction and finishing trades (13.1%) and mechanical engineering (5.3%). Compared with a year earlier, industrial production climbed 2.3%, recovering from a 1.8% fall in July.

Corn Futures Rise on US Harvest Concerns — Corn futures rose toward $5.1 per bushel, hitting a one-week high as concerns over delayed US harvesting and weaker crop conditions supported prices. Only 23% of the US corn crop had been harvested by October 4, below the 27% five-year average and 26% expected by analysts, after heavy Midwest rainfall delayed fieldwork. The share of the crop rated good-to-excellent also fell to 54% from 57% a week earlier. Meanwhile, export demand remains relatively firm, with 2026/27 shipments running 4.4% above last year at a record pace for this point in the marketing year, despite weekly inspections falling to 1.368 million tonnes. Elsewhere, Brazil’s first corn crop was 38% planted, slightly behind 40% a year earlier. Traders now await Friday’s USDA report for updated production and yield estimates, with current forecasts at 178.5 bushels per acre and 15.8 billion bushels of output.

【CNPC Huabei oilfield posts reserve peak and production growth, bolstering energy security】China National Petroleum Corp (CNPC) said the Bayan oilfield in the Hetao Basin, Inner Mongolia, reached 5,080 t/d of crude — the first time above 5,000 t/d and a record high — marking a step-up in reserves and output in CNPC’s Huabei oilfield new area. CNPC engineer Liu Jing said technology-led exploration, including a late-stage rapid-subsidence basin theory and breakthroughs in deep continental clastic reservoirs, delivered western Inner Mongolia’s first million-tonne-per-year oilfield. Huabei’s crude output has held at the c.5 mln tonnes level for two consecutive years and remained stable; through end-September Huabei produced 3.86 mln tonnes YTD, with Bayan up 14% YoY and accounting for nearly one-third of Huabei’s daily output. CNPC said the reserve additions and output gains provide replicable experience for boosting production in mature onshore oilfield new areas.

【Shenzhen property market during National Day: price no longer decisive】Field checks across Shenzhen's Longgang and Longhua districts during the National Day holiday show developers stepping up incentives—appliance giveaways, discounts and cash rebates—while viewing volumes remain roughly in line with prior years. Sales staff report visitor numbers rose but transactions did not, with longer decision cycles and heavier price comparison; market is bifurcated—a few projects stay active but most cooled versus late September. Buyers are prioritizing delivery certainty and product quality; price is no longer the sole deciding factor. Leyoujia Research Center data: Oct 1–5 Shenzhen new-home contracts +9% YoY, resale contracts +11% YoY. On Oct 1, one-day first-home viewings rose over 169.3% sequentially and signings rose 160% sequentially, with visits at the highest level since mid-September. Li Yujia, chief researcher at Guangdong Housing Policy Research Center, says Shenzhen is likely experiencing a structural 'Golden October'—transaction volumes may rebound sequentially but a simultaneous rise in prices is unlikely; demand will concentrate in high-value, well-located, high-quality projects, while a shortage of standout inventory versus abundant average stock persists.

US Natural Gas Prices Extend Gains — US natural gas prices climbed to around $3.13 per MMBtu, extending gains for a fourth session and reaching their highest level since September 25, supported by falling production, LNG feedgas flows, and expectations of more demand. Average gas output in the US Lower 48 states fell to 111.7 bcfd so far in October, down from record highs of 113.3 bcfd in both August and September, while daily production continued to drop partly due to recent force majeure events and other disruptions affecting pipelines in Kentucky, Texas, and West Virginia. LNG demand also provided support, with daily feedgas flows rising as Freeport LNG in Texas ramps up gas intake following the return of a liquefaction train from maintenance. Meanwhile, forecasts shifted warmer, with above-average temperatures expected across the western two-thirds of the US through October 10, which could lift cooling demand and gas consumption for power generation.

### 2026-10-07 10:46:59  #Trading Economics #Economy #Fiji #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Fiji Inflation Eases From 15-Year High — Fiji’s annual inflation slowed to 6.8% in September 2026 from 7.6% in the previous month, which was the highest since December 2011. The moderation came as food inflation eased to 7.6% from a 28-month high of 9.5% in August, while housing inflation eased to a four-month low of 4.9% from 7.0% in August. Inflation also moderated for miscellaneous goods and services (3.1% vs 3.6%) and furnishings, household equipment, and routine household maintenance (0.1% vs 0.4%), while remaining flat for clothing and footwear (0.0% vs. 1.2%). Meanwhile, inflation accelerated for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (13.9% vs 13.3%) and transport (12.2% vs 11.7%), while education inflation was steady at 0.4%. Additionally, recreation and culture prices fell at a faster pace (-1.5% vs -1.4%). On a monthly basis, the CPI fell 0.4% in September, following a 0.1% decline in August and marking the third straight monthly decline.

### 2026-10-07 10:44:42  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Soybeans Rebound on Brazil Planting Delays — Soybean futures climbed toward $13.1 per bushel, bouncing off from multi-week lows as slower planting in Brazil and signs of continued export demand lifted prices. About 7.3% of soybean crop in Brazil, the world’s largest exporter, had been planted as of Thursday, below 9% a year earlier, with consultancy AgRural estimating 2026/27 production at 182.7 million metric tons. The USDA has a higher forecast of 186 million tons, which would exceed last year’s record 180.5 million tons. On the demand side, an unnamed buyer purchased 104,000 tonnes of US soybeans, while US export inspections reached 3.99 million tonnes since the start of September, up from 3.05 million tonnes a year earlier. However, gains remained limited by expectations for a large US crop and weak Chinese demand. China’s soybean purchases are being constrained by high inventories, poor crushing margins and an additional 10% tariff on US soybeans, while private buyers continue to favor supplies from Brazil and Argentina.

Hong Kong Stocks Retreat — The Hang Seng Index declined 0.6%, or 145 points, to 24,134 on Wednesday, reversing part of previous session's rebound, as renewed gains in oil prices revived inflation and interest-rate concerns. Brent crude rose above US$101 a barrel after a storm threatened US Gulf oil facilities and Iran-backed Houthis launched attacks on targets in Saudi Arabia, raising concerns over global energy supplies. The rise in oil prices offset positive cues from Wall Street, while markets continued to scale back expectations for another Federal Reserve rate hike this month. Hong Kong trading also remained subdued as mainland Chinese markets and Southbound Stock Connect stayed closed for the National Day holiday, limiting mainland buying support. Healthcare and technology stocks, which led Tuesday’s rebound, came under renewed selling pressure, with Tencent (-0.8%), Wuxi Biologics (-3.3%), Genscript Biotech (-14.4%), Xiaomi (-1.1%), and InSilico Medicine Cayman (-8.7%) among the notable decliners.

Gasoline Rises for Second Day — US gasoline futures rose above $3.30 per gallon, gaining for the second straight session, as a storm in the Gulf of Mexico threatened to disrupt refinery operations. Refineries in US Gulf states account for about half of US refining capacity, which stands at 18.2 million barrels per day. Meanwhile, continued attacks in the Middle East kept energy-market risks elevated despite improvement in regional shipments. Russia’s ongoing restrictions on diesel exports and refinery disruptions, alongside China’s reported suspension of some October fuel exports, also tightened fuel markets. Gasoline inventories were 7% below the five-year average as of September 25, according to the EIA, while refinery utilization stood at 92.5%. The EIA also raised its 2026 and 2027 oil price forecasts, citing rapidly falling global inventories and supply risks. Separately, the G7’s planned release of emergency crude and diesel reserves could provide some relief.

### 2026-10-07 09:46:37  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Currency #Importance 1

Australian Dollar Halts 3-Day Rally — The Australian dollar held near $0.70 after a three-session rally as investors awaited key central bank updates for clues on the policy outlook. The Federal Reserve is due to release minutes from its September meeting and hear from several policymakers this week, while markets are pricing in around an 80% chance of no rate change this month following a weaker-than-expected labor market report. Meanwhile, the Aussie found some support as the US dollar weakened broadly, with its biggest decline against the euro as stress in European bond markets eased. In Australia, consumer sentiment slumped for a second straight month in October as higher borrowing costs squeezed household finances and added to cost-of-living pressures. Markets now see a 75.7% chance that the RBA will leave rates unchanged in November, versus a 24.3% chance of another hike. Attention will also turn to the RBA’s meeting minutes next week for further policy signals.

【中國團隊全球首次構建水稻全生命週期三維時空細胞圖譜】記者從崖州灣國家實驗室獲悉，由我國多個科研機構分工協作完成的最新水稻研究成果，於6日晚在國際學術期刊《細胞》（Cell）線上發表。內容為我國研究團隊在全球範圍內首次構建了覆蓋水稻從種子萌發到開花結實全過程的三維時空細胞圖譜，整合了基因組測序、單細胞測序與空間轉錄組測序，為理解水稻生長發育、探索籽粒品質改良提供了新的研究資源。（央視新聞）

### 2026-10-07 09:23:07  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy

【A$70 billion Australian coal sector faces new climate-law risk after High Court ruling】Australia’s High Court on Wednesday ruled in a major coal mine expansion case that a local planning authority failed to properly consider measures to limit greenhouse gas emissions. A lower court had found scope 3 emissions from customers burning the coal would account for about 98% of the project’s potential pollution. The decision will directly affect New South Wales coal mine approvals and could serve as a precedent for similar projects nationwide; roughly 30 new or expanded coal projects are seeking government sign-off. Australia’s coal export sector is about A$70 billion (US$49 billion); miners warn approval delays could hit jobs and damage Australia’s standing as an energy supplier.

### 2026-10-07 09:15:59  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Silver Slips as Oil Prices Rebound — Silver slipped below $61 an ounce on Wednesday, paring gains from the previous session as oil prices rebounded on persistent risks to Middle East supply, keeping inflationary risks and rate hike concerns in focus. Oil prices advanced as Iran intensified attacks on tankers in the Strait of Hormuz in recent days, while Saudi forces continued to clash with the Houthis. Treasury yields also remained near multi-decade highs amid concerns over persistent inflation, rising fiscal risks and elevated AI-related debt issuances. Meanwhile, markets are pricing in nearly an 80% chance that the US Federal Reserve will keep rates unchanged this month following weaker-than-expected job growth in September and downward revisions to payrolls for the prior two months. Investors now await minutes from the Fed’s latest meeting for further clues on the monetary policy outlook.

### 2026-10-07 09:14:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #Philippines #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Philippines Manufacturing Output Growth Picks Up in August — Manufacturing production in the Philippines increased by 10.8% year-on-year in August, accelerating from an upwardly revised 9.9% rise in the previous month. The largest positive contribution came from the manufacture of computer, electronic and optical products (30.1% vs 15.8%), which accounted for 57.3% of the acceleration in the annual growth rate. Other major contributors included the manufacture of transport equipment (11.5% vs 4.9%), basic metals (27.2% vs 23.9%), rubber and plastic products (15.0% vs 9.9%), electrical equipment (10.9% vs 2.9%), and beverages (4.2% vs 3.6%). Meanwhile, production growth moderated for coke and refined petroleum products (24.7% vs 47.6%) and food products (1.3% vs 2.6%). For the January-August period, manufacturing output was 9.3% higher than in the same period a year earlier.

### 2026-10-07 09:14:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #Philippines #Unemployment Rate #Importance 2

Philippines Jobless Rate Falls to 5.3% — The unemployment rate in the Philippines fell to 5.3% in August 2026 from a four-year high of 6% in the previous month. The number of unemployed persons declined to 2.77 million from 3.14 million, while the employment rate rose to 94.7% from 94% and the number of employed persons increased to 49.79 million from 49.21 million over the same period. The underemployment rate also eased to 11.9% from 12.9%, with 5.90 million workers seeking additional hours or another job. On a year-on-year basis, however, the unemployment rate remained above 3.9% in August 2025, while the employment rate was below 96.1% and the number of employed persons was down from 50.10 million. The underemployment rate was also higher than 10.7% a year earlier. The services sector remained the largest employer, accounting for 61.7% of total employment, followed by agriculture at 20.9% and industry at 17.3%. Average weekly working hours fell to 39.2 from 40.6 in July and 41.0 a year earlier.

### 2026-10-07 09:13:25  #宏觀 #市場 #國際

【貝森特執掌下的美國財政部：內部緊張、人事動盪及未達成的經濟目標】據媒體報道，知情人士稱，在1月財政部的一場會議上，美國財長貝森特當面質問負責恐怖主義與金融情報事務的財政部副部長約翰・赫爾利，當著多名下屬的面大聲斥責，口出穢言。貝森特音量極大，附近辦公的員工都能聽見。訊息人士表示，幾周之後，貝森特便和幕僚商議，打算為這位已經獲得參議院確認任職的赫爾利另尋一份財政部之外的崗位。赫爾利後來調任美國駐經濟合作與發展組織大使。彼時，財長正面臨職業生涯中最嚴峻的挑戰，赫爾利的離職也成了財政部高層一連串人事出走中的一例。知情人士稱，貝森特的行事風格是多名核心官員離職的誘因，有時甚至是最主要原因。據十餘名財政部現任及前任官員透露，自2025年初就任財長以來，無論事情大小，貝森特既斥責高階顧問，也訓斥基層工作人員。但特朗普反而進一步倚重貝森特，授權他去化解多項難題，包括借貸成本高企、受能源價格推動而難以遏制的通脹，以及政府債務快速累積的困境。與此同時，貝森特還受命透過一項名為“經濟棄兒行動”的計劃，以經濟手段終結對伊朗的戰爭。一名曾與貝森特共事的政府高階官員表示，總統整套經濟政策的重擔全部壓在這位財長身上。該官員稱：“斯科特輸不起。”貝森特曾建議特朗普推行“3-3-3”政策：到2028年將預算赤字降至國內生產總值的3%、推動國內生產總值實現3%增長、每日增產300萬桶石油。但實際經濟增速僅約2%，自特朗普上任以來石油日增產量不足100萬桶。美國負責任聯邦預算委員會資料顯示，當前預算赤字佔國內生產總值比重超過6%，美國債務總量近期突破40萬億美元。

【青花汾20六連陽迭創新高 精品茅臺強勢反彈 飛天茅臺零售均價降至1799】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，10月7日中國白酒業12大單品的終端零售均價五漲三跌四平，平盤品種數量明顯增多，但上漲品種數量仍佔優，且累計漲幅超過下跌端，整體價盤延續回升態勢。贏家方面，精品茅臺上漲3元至2467元，強勢收復昨日失地，再度向2470元一線靠攏；青花汾20上漲2元至415元，六連陽強勢攀升，再創近一個月新高；古井貢古20上漲1元至553元，三連陽穩步走強，繼續重新整理近30天最高紀錄；習酒君品上漲3元至636元，結束此前三連跌；水晶劍南春上漲1元至415元，止住昨日回撥，價格重新走高。輸家方面，飛天茅臺下跌2元至1799元，此前連續17日挺立1800元關口之上；洋河夢之藍M6+下跌1元至617元，四連跌持續回探，自近期高點進一步回落；青花郎下跌1元至698元，昨日反彈後再度轉跌，重新落至700元關口下方。持平方面，茅臺1935維持688元不變，昨日回落後價格暫時企穩；五糧液普五八代保持806元不變，連續第二日平盤，已連續四天穩定在800元之上；五糧液1618維持830元，近期高位執行態勢未改；國窖1573保持882元不變，昨日大漲後進入高位整理。

今日12大單品的終端零售總價繼續回升，4款產品價格為平盤，而贏家累計漲幅仍佔優，整體價盤延續修復走勢。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10806元，較昨日溫和上漲6元，連續第二日站穩10800元整百關口，並創下5天以來新高。

### 2026-10-07 09:08:23  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China

【China's railways expect 24.15 million passengers today】China's railways expect to carry 24.15 million passengers today, the final day of the National Day–Mid-Autumn holiday, after return flows rose from yesterday. Holiday rail services began Sept 23; through yesterday more than 250 million passenger trips have been handled. With the return peak, capacity has been increased: 2,449 additional passenger trains are scheduled today. China Railway Beijing Group expects volumes to be up 15% YoY and plans to add 169.5 train pairs, mainly on routes to Harbin, Shenyang, Dalian and Datong. China Railway Guangzhou Group expects volumes up 20.8% YoY and plans 765 additional passenger trains, mainly on Beijing–Guangzhou, Guangzhou–Shenzhen–Hong Kong, Hangzhou–Shenzhen and Jiangzhan routes.
